# Progress log — Morocco Tours Hub

Dated log of every page and change (moved out of CLAUDE.md on 2026-11-19 to save tokens). Add one "Done/Update" paragraph at the END for each new change, with "Owner to confirm" items. Search this file (grep) for a fact; do not read it whole.

## Progress log (homepage redesign)
**Done (2026-11-10/11) — header + hero top, homepage only (`index.html`):**
- **Overlay header**: `<header class="site-header site-header--overlay">` sits on the hero photo (transparent, white links, gold active underline). On scroll (`main.js` toggles `.is-scrolled` at >10px) a **black** bar fades in (`::before`, opacity only). Other pages still use the old white sticky header.
- **Logo**: homepage uses `assets/images/logo-white.png` — generated from `logo.png`: word MOROCCO + man + tagline + swoosh white; **camels & palm trees stay dark**; gold star, lantern and gold streaks removed from the word (first O replaced by a copy of a clean O; M has a few tiny gaps — a designer file would be cleaner). Originals `logo.png` / `logo-light.png` untouched. Owner rejected: cream-badge behind logo, cream-lettering version.
- **Header actions** (right side, menu centred): round outlined **WhatsApp icon** (`.header-wa`) + solid gold square-ish **CONTACT US** block (`.header-contact`, uppercase). Hidden ≤767px (owner, 2026-11-12: no header icons on phones; the floating WhatsApp button covers it); mobile drawer got a green "WhatsApp Us" button under "Book Your Tour". Tightened nav spacing 1181–1320px so it fits.
- **Hero buttons**: `.hero2__actions` = gold **EXPLORE OUR TOURS** (car icon) + dark navy glass **CHAT ON WHATSAPP**; compact, radius 6px, uppercase; stack full-width ≤420px.
- Hero top scrim darkened slightly for header legibility.
- **Site-wide CSS fixes** (all pages): burger was pushed off-screen on phones (header Book button hidden ≤520px, `.header-tools` margin-left:auto ≤1180px); footer caused sideways scroll on phones (single column ≤520px); hero side padding restored (`.hero2__grid` uses padding-top/bottom only).

**Done (2026-11-11) — hero trust strip:** old `.hero2__bar` (3 features + Daily Departures/Book Now) replaced by `.hero2__trust`: solid navy-deep band, gold gradient hairline on top, 5 items (gold line icon + Poppins uppercase title + muted line) — Private Tours, English-Speaking Guides, Pick-up Service, Flexible Payment, Book with Confidence. 5 cols → 3 cols ≤1100px → stacked list ≤640px. Owner's layout reference was the agency site's dark 5-icon bar.
**Hero sized to the reference (measured at 1366×768):** desktop ≥1025px uses a wide container (max 1480px, 56px side padding) for the overlay header, hero and trust strip, so logo/headline/strip all start 56px from the edge like the reference. Hero fills the first screen (`min-height: calc(100svh - 144px)`), trust strip top at ~700px. Title ~53px (was 80), sub ~24px, body 15px/480px wide, smaller avatars card. Hero image fixed to `loading="eager" fetchpriority="high"`.
**Pixel-matched to the reference at 1366×768 (2026-11-11):** desktop container = 1280px max with side padding `clamp(20px, …, 56px)` so content edge is always ≥56px. Overlay header 116px tall (16px top padding → items centred at y=66), logo 68px tall at (56,32), menu uppercase .86rem starting x=329, WhatsApp 40px at 1107, CONTACT US 129×45 ending x=1295; scrolled bar shrinks to 76px / 54px logo. Hero fills the whole first screen (100svh) like the owner's own-browser view of the reference; text block anchored 80px above the photo bottom; trust strip appears just below the fold on the first scroll, icons at 56/310/564/819/1073 (5 cols, 32px gap, dividers drawn in the gap). Social icons flush-left in 44px slots from y=296 ("Follow us" label kept for screen readers only).

## Secrets & git
- **Any API key goes in `.env`, which is listed in `.gitignore`. Never put a key in a page file, never commit it to GitHub.** `.env.example` holds placeholder names only.
- Don't commit or push unless the user asks.
- The original export is kept as `../morocco-tours-hub-website.zip` (backup).

**Done (2026-11-17) — 18-day Atlantic Morocco tour (Marrakech → Dakhla) rebuilt from research as an expert coast route** (`tours/18-day-atlantic-morocco-tour-marrakech-to-dakhla.html`, URL kept; old page read once; notes in the build script header; SERP read with WebSearch, the OpenSEO project id was not found this session so no keyword volumes). Page 1 has no private Marrakech → Dakhla tour: coast tours stop at Mirleft/Legzira/Sidi Ifni or Tan-Tan Plage. **Safety checked:** UK FCDO (updated 29 Jun 2026, still current 17 Nov 2026) advises against all travel only within 40 km north/west of the Berm (incl. Es Semara) and beyond it; the coastal N1 (Laayoune, Boujdour, Dakhla) is far from it; Sahara Overland (Nov 2026) calls the N1 straightforward and safe, with frequent checkpoints; avoid night driving → FAQ + Good to know (passport, daylight only, check insurance). Old page had a 20 km Taghazout → Agadir day and two empty nights in Tan-Tan. **New route (OSRM km):** D1 Marrakech airport any hour · D2 guided Marrakech · D3 → Essaouira · D4 free in Essaouira · D5 Imsouane → Taghazout (169 km) · D6 free in Taghazout (surf lesson / Paradise Valley) · D7 Agadir Oufella → Aït Baha → Tafraoute (183 km) · D8 Tafraoute: painted rocks, Ammeln Valley, Aït Mansour gorge · D9 Tiznit → Mirleft (148 km) · D10 Legzira + Sidi Ifni (~70 km) · D11 Guelmim → Tan-Tan → Akhfennir on the Khnifiss lagoon (370 km, ~5 h) · D12 Tarfaya (Saint-Exupéry museum, Casa del Mar) → Laayoune (202 km) · D13 Boujdour → Dakhla (532 km, ~7 h) · D14 White Dune + oyster farm by 4×4 · D15 kite/wing/kayak or rest · D16 4×4 along the open coast · D17 free · D18 Dakhla airport. Title "18-Day Atlantic Morocco Tour: Marrakech to Dakhla | Private", h1 "18-Day Atlantic Morocco Tour to Dakhla" + script "Where the Sahara Meets the Ocean", hero essaouira-skala-port.jpg (only 1000px wide, a bit soft on laptops), chip Marrakech, route "Marrakech → Dakhla". Key fact 4 **"Dakhla: 5 nights by the lagoon"**. Included: vehicle, driver-guide, local guide in Marrakech, Marrakech + Dakhla airport transfers, 17 nights, 17 breakfasts (no dinners), **two 4×4 days around Dakhla**, fuel + tolls. Costs: lunches, Bahia 100 + Saadian Tombs 100 MAD, Skala du Port small fee, Saint-Exupéry museum small fee if open, "All other sites are free", tips. Accommodations one row per night (Booking.com search links for the new places; my research picks, owner to confirm): Marrakech + Essaouira = usual places · **Taghazout: World of Waves Surf House / Radisson Blu Surf Village (TA 4.5/2,215) / Fairmont Taghazout Bay** · **Tafraoute: Hotel Salama / Hotel Les Amandiers / Chez Amaliya** · **Mirleft: Auberge Dar Fayçal / Dar Najmat / Kasbah Tabelkoukt** · **Akhfennir: La Courbine d’Argent (one cell across all levels; 9.1/40, the one good inn)** · **Laayoune: Sahara Line Hotel / Hotel Parador (one Midrange + Luxury cell: no 5-star in town)** · **Dakhla: Kite House Dakhla / Dakhla Attitude / Palais Rhoul & Spa**. FAQ: is the road to Dakhla safe · how long are the drives · flying home from Dakhla (RAM to Casablanca, direct Paris-Orly + Madrid; we check times) · five days in Dakhla · do we need to surf in Taghazout · booking. **Done (2026-11-17) — 7-day Coastal Morocco tour (Marrakech round trip) rebuilt from research** (`tours/7-day-coastal-morocco-tour-from-marrakech.html`, URL kept; old page read once; notes in the build script header; OpenSEO out of credits, SERP read with WebSearch + Firecrawl). Page 1: no 7-day private tour with this exact route; TourRadar 8-day private Marrakech → Essaouira → Imsouane → Taghazout → Agadir → Tafraoute, a 7-day Marrakech coast loop (Essaouira, Taghazout, Paradise Valley, Agadir), Fodor's 7-day southern coast (~US$1,470–2,500 pp). Timlalin dunes = just past Tamri on the N1, ~68 km from Agadir, high above the sea (sandboarding tours from Agadir/Taghazout). Imsouane: no ATM, cash only. Old page had Essaouira → Imsouane "~180 km" (OSRM 110 km) and two nights in Agadir with a free day. **Expert choices:** two nights in Taghazout (Paradise Valley morning included by car + surf/cliffs afternoon) and one in Agadir; Sidi Kaouki beach on the way to Imsouane. Route: D1 Marrakech hotel → Essaouira (~180 km) · D2 free in Essaouira · D3 Sidi Kaouki → Imsouane (110 km) · D4 Timlalin dunes → Taghazout (~75 km) · D5 Paradise Valley + Taghazout bay · D6 → Agadir (Oufella hill, Souk El Had, promenade) · D7 motorway → Marrakech hotel/airport by early afternoon (~250 km; Agadir airport on request). Key fact 1 "Marrakech round trip", key fact 4 "Coast: 6 nights by the ocean". Vehicle private A/C car or minivan. Included 6 nights + 6 breakfasts, Paradise Valley and Timlalin by car, fuel/tolls/parking; not included surf lessons and sandboard hire. Costs: lunches, Skala du Port small fee, Agadir Oufella cable car "a ticket, if you ride it", "All other sites are free", tips. Good to know: no ATM in Imsouane, cool ocean wind, strong currents (swim in sheltered bays), Paradise Valley pools lower after a dry summer. Accommodations one row per stop: Essaouira (Riad Baladin / Madada Mogador / L’Heure Bleue Palais) · **Imsouane: Imsouane Magic House / Windhouse (one Midrange + Luxury cell, no luxury hotel in the village)** · Taghazout (World of Waves / Radisson Blu Surf Village / Fairmont) · **Agadir: Hyatt Place / Royal Atlas Hotel & Spa / Sofitel Agadir Thalassa** — my picks, owner to confirm (Booking search links). Facts checked: **drones** — Morocco has banned recreational drones since 2015 and airport customs confiscate them (uavcoach, drone-traveller, bladi.net); **portraits** — ask first, Jemaa el-Fnaa performers expect a few dirhams; fees as on the finished pages (Hassan II 140 MAD, no visits Friday; Volubilis 70–100; Bou Inania ~20; Taourirt small fee or Atlas studios ~80 MAD). **Done (2026-11-17) — Merzouga activities, page 6 (last): `tours/merzouga-medfouna-cooking-class.html` (NEW).** Built from the 4×4 day page (`build-mzcook.js`). Medfouna/madfouna = the "Berber pizza" of the Merzouga–Rissani–Erfoud area, name from the Arabic for "buried": dough sealed around minced meat, onion, herbs and spices, baked buried in hot sand under the embers (Atlas Obscura, MarocMama, Kiss from the World); Merzouga classes sell at ~US$30 (3.5 h, 26 five-star ratings) to ~US$59 (private ~4 h). h1 "Medfouna Cooking Class in Merzouga" + "The Berber pizza, baked in the sand", booking label "Private cooking class", form field "Class", hero facts About 3.5 hours / Where: a family home in Merzouga. Key facts: Times 10:00 or 16:00 start · Hands-on dough to filling, yourself · Baked in the hot sand · Meal you eat what you make · Diets vegetarian on request · Cancellation. Programme 6 steps (welcome tea → dough → filling → seal + bury ~30 min → salad + mint-tea pour → eat together). Good to know: halal, no pork or alcohol; children welcome. FAQ: what is medfouna · cook or watch · vegetarian · is sand-baking clean · morning or afternoon · booking. Make It Your Own: Rissani market shopping, vegetable tagine, sunset ride, cook at the camp, add the 4×4 day, group class. Think twice → Marrakech cooking class / 4×4 day.
